40b7(rgyud): gser gyi rnga dbyangs dam pathos gyur na//sdug bsngal spangs nas thar pa thob par gyur//da lta du stobs bcu'i 'jig rten nas//rtag tu bzhugs pa rkang gnyis pa ci//thugs rje 
41a1(rgyud): @#//chen po'i thugs kyis ni//brtse bas bdag la dgongs su gsol//sems can gang zhig skyabs ma mchis//mgon ni bgyid pa ma mchis pa/
41a2(rgyud): de lta bu yi rnam pa la//skyabs chen dag ni bgyid par shog/bdag gis sngon ni bgyis pa'i sdig/las ngan shin tu lji ba rnams//deng bdag stobs bcu'i spyan sngar ni 
41a3(rgyud): thams cad rtse gcig rab tu bshags//bdag gis sangs rgyas yid ma che//bla ma nye bshes ma gus dang //dge ba mang po ma spyad pas//rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa 'am//
41a4(rgyud): bdag gis mtho btsun rus rigs dang //longs spyod dag gi snyems pa dang //dar la bab pas dregs gyur nas//rtag tu las ngan bgyid pa 'am//rtag tu log pa'i bsam 
41a5(rgyud): bskyed nas//ngag gis tshig ngan smras pa dang //kha na mtho ma mthong nas//rtag tu las ngan gyis pa 'am//rtag tu byis pa'i blo spyad pas//ma rig mun pa'i sems 
41a6(rgyud): g.yogs dang //mi dge'i grogs po'i rjes 'brangs nas//rtag tu las ngan 'gyis pa 'am//rtsed mo dga' ba rnams kyi rgyas//mya ngan dag dang ldan 'gyur zhing /

41b1(rgyud): dang //dbul phongs gyu gyu spyad pas na//de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am//sdig mang dag la mi mos kyang //'jigs skrag dang dang bcas pa 'am//rang dbang dag ni ma mchis pas//
41b2(rgyud): de slad sdig rnams bgyis ba 'am//rnam par g.yer ba'i bsam pa dang //khong khro khon du 'dzin pa dang //bkres sgom dag gis gdungs pa'i rgyus//de slad sdig rnams pa gyis pa

41b4(rgyud): 'dun la//gus pa'i sems ni ma skyes pa'i//de 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa//deng bdag thams cad so sor bshags//rang rgyal byang chub sems dpa' la//gus pa'i 
41b5(rgyud): sems ni ma mchis pa'i//de 'dra'i sdig dang gang bgyis pa//deng dag thams cad so sor bshegs//mi shes dam chos skur ba dang //pha ma sri zhu ma bgyis pa'i//de 'dra'i

41b7(rgyud): thams cad so sor bshags /bdag gis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams//sangs rgyas blangs mang gang dag mchod//sems can rnams ni rab dwangs nas//sdug bsngal 
42a1(rgyud): @#//bgegs rnams bral bgyid shog/sems can dag ni thams cad kun//sa bcu la ni gnas gyur te//bsod nams ye shes yongs rdzogs nas//

42a3(rgyud): shog/bdag gis srog chags kun gyi slad//gser 'od mchog tu rnam par rgyal//zab mo'i mdo sde bshad nas ni//las ngan rnams ni bsal bar shog/gang zhig bskal pa brgya

42b1(rgyud): tshogs pa thob par shog/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kun//bdag la so sor gzigs dgongs nas//thugs rje chen po'i thugs kyis ni//bdag gi bshags pa

42b7(rgyud): 'chad par yongs mi bgyid//lus gsum ngag gi rnam pa bzhi//yid kyi las ni rnam pa gsum//sems can rnams ni 'ching bgyid pas//thog ma med nas rgyun mi 'chad//
43a1(rgyud): @#//spyod pa 'di gsum dbang gis na//mi dge las bcu bgyid gyur bas//de 'dra'i nongs pa mang po rnams//de dag thams cad so sor bshags//bdag

43a4(rgyud): te//sa bcu dag la rab tu gnas//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas rtag mthong smon//bdag gis lus dang ngag yid kyis//bsod nams ye shes las bsgrubs pa'i//dge

43a6(rgyud): byis pa srid gsum rmongs pa'i bgegs dag dang //rtag par las rang na lci ba bgyis pa'i bgegs//bdag gis log pa'i 'dod pa bsags pa'i bgegs//rtag tu 
43a7(rgyud): sred skyed 'khor bar 'gyur ba'i bgegs//'jig rten 'di la mngon zhen chags pa'i bgegs//byis pa thams cad kyi nyon mongs bgegs//sems myos rnams g.yeng 
43b1(rgyud): phyin ci log pa'i bgegs//mi dge grogs po dag la rten pa'i bgegs//'khor ba dag la 'dod chags zhen pa'i bgegs//zhe sdang gti mug glen pas sdig bgyis bgegs//mi kho 
43b2(rgyud): brgyad skye mi dge'i grogs po'i bgegs//bsod nams yon tan ma bsags pa yi bgegs//reng bdag rgyal ba kun gyi spyan sngar ni//sdig pa'i las ni mtha' yas bgyis pa

44a1(rgyud): @#//dag gis rab tu rgyan//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal chu bo'i nang dag tu//rga dag mya ngan chu yis dad pas na//de 'dra'i sdug bsngal rgya mtsho bzod dka' ga

44b1(rgyud): ci mtshos pas//myur du tha nam mtshos gtso grub nas//yang dag chos bshad skye dgu phan btags te//thams cad sdug bsngal mang las thar bgyid shog/bdud kyi dmag tshogs

48a1(rgyud): @#//pa de yig bcu thal mo sbyar nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol blo/bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis
